Claude: ## Step 3: Process CSV in Google Colab

```
# Complete processing script for DOB Permit Issuance
from google.colab import files
import pandas as pd

# Upload the fresh CSV file
print("Upload your DOB_Permit_Issuance CSV file:")
uploaded = files.upload()

# Get filename
filename = list(uploaded.keys())[0]
print(f"Processing file: {filename}")

# Read CSV in chunks due to size
print("Reading large CSV file...")
df_list = []
chunk_size = 50000

for chunk in pd.read_csv(filename, chunksize=chunk_size, dtype=str, low_memory=False):
    df_list.append(chunk)
    print(f"Read chunk with {len(chunk)} rows")

# Combine all chunks
df = pd.concat(df_list, ignore_index=True)
print(f"Total rows loaded: {len(df)}")

# Show original column names
print("\nOriginal column names:")
for i, col in enumerate(df.columns):
    print(f"{i}: {col}")

# Deduplicate - keep most recent permit per BIN
print("\nDeduplicating...")
df['Issuance Date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['Issuance Date'], errors='coerce')
df_sorted = df.sort_values(['Bin #', 'Issuance Date'], ascending=[True, False])
df_latest = df_sorted.drop_duplicates(subset=['Bin #'], keep='first')

print(f"After deduplication: {len(df_latest)} rows")
print(f"Reduction: {len(df) - len(df_latest)} rows removed")

# Map to snake_case column names for Supabase
column_mapping = {
    'BOROUGH': 'borough',
    'Bin #': 'bin_number',
    'House #': 'house_number',
    'Street Name': 'street_name',
    'Job #': 'job_number',
    'Job doc. #': 'job_doc_number',
    'Job Type': 'job_type',
    'Self_Cert': 'self_cert',
    'Block': 'block_text',
    'Lot': 'lot_text',
    'Community Board': 'community_board',
    'Zip Code': 'zip_code',
    'Bldg Type': 'bldg_type',
    'Residential': 'residential',
    'Special District 1': 'special_district_1',
    'Special District 2': 'special_district_2',
    'Work Type': 'work_type',
    'Permit Status': 'permit_status',
    'Filing Status': 'filing_status',
    'Permit Type': 'permit_type',

## While That Downloads, Let's Create a Processing Script

```
cat > process_permits.py << 'EOF'
import pandas as pd
import sys

print("Processing DOB Permit Issuance data...")

# Read CSV in chunks
print("Reading large CSV file...")
df_list = []
chunk_size = 50000

try:
    for chunk in pd.read_csv('DOB_Permit_Issuance_Fresh.csv', chunksize=chunk_size, dtype=str, low_memory=False):
        df_list.append(chunk)
        print(f"Read chunk with {len(chunk)} rows")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"Error reading file: {e}")
    sys.exit(1)

# Combine chunks
df = pd.concat(df_list, ignore_index=True)
print(f"Total rows: {len(df)}")

# Deduplicate
print("Deduplicating by BIN...")
df['Issuance Date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['Issuance Date'], errors='coerce')
df_sorted = df.sort_values(['Bin #', 'Issuance Date'], ascending=[True, False])
df_latest = df_sorted.drop_duplicates(subset=['Bin #'], keep='first')
print(f"After deduplication: {len(df_latest)} rows")

# Map column names to snake_case
column_mapping = {
    'BOROUGH': 'borough',
    'Bin #': 'bin_number',
    'House #': 'house_number',
    'Street Name': 'street_name',
    'Job #': 'job_number',
    'Job doc. #': 'job_doc_number',
    'Job Type': 'job_type',
    'Self_Cert': 'self_cert',
    'Block': 'block_text',
    'Lot': 'lot_text',
    'Community Board': 'community_board',
    'Zip Code': 'zip_code',
    'Bldg Type': 'bldg_type',
    'Residential': 'residential',
    'Special District 1': 'special_district_1',
    'Special District 2': 'special_district_2',
    'Work Type': 'work_type',
    'Permit Status': 'permit_status',
    'Filing Status': 'filing_status',
    'Permit Type': 'permit_type',
    'Permit Sequence #': 'permit_sequence_number',
    'Permit Subtype': 'permit_subtype',
    'Oil Gas': 'oil_gas',
    'Site Fill': 'site_fill',
    'Filing Date': 'filing_date',

Claude: ## Create the Processing Script

```
cat > process_permits.py << 'EOF'
import pandas as pd
import sys

print("Processing DOB Permit Issuance data...")

# Read CSV in chunks
print("Reading large CSV file...")
df_list = []
chunk_size = 50000

try:
    for chunk in pd.read_csv('DOB_Permit_Issuance_Fresh.csv', chunksize=chunk_size, dtype=str, low_memory=False):
        df_list.append(chunk)
        print(f"Read chunk with {len(chunk)} rows")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"Error reading file: {e}")
    sys.exit(1)

# Combine chunks
df = pd.concat(df_list, ignore_index=True)
print(f"Total rows: {len(df)}")

# Deduplicate
print("Deduplicating by BIN...")
df['Issuance Date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['Issuance Date'], errors='coerce')
df_sorted = df.sort_values(['Bin #', 'Issuance Date'], ascending=[True, False])
df_latest = df_sorted.drop_duplicates(subset=['Bin #'], keep='first')
print(f"After deduplication: {len(df_latest)} rows")

# Map column names to snake_case
column_mapping = {
    'BOROUGH': 'borough',
    'Bin #': 'bin_number',
    'House #': 'house_number',
    'Street Name': 'street_name',
    'Job #': 'job_number',
    'Job doc. #': 'job_doc_number',
    'Job Type': 'job_type',
    'Self_Cert': 'self_cert',
    'Block': 'block_text',
    'Lot': 'lot_text',
    'Community Board': 'community_board',
    'Zip Code': 'zip_code',
    'Bldg Type': 'bldg_type',
    'Residential': 'residential',
    'Special District 1': 'special_district_1',
    'Special District 2': 'special_district_2',
    'Work Type': 'work_type',
    'Permit Status': 'permit_status',
    'Filing Status': 'filing_status',
    'Permit Type': 'permit_type',
    'Permit Sequence #': 'permit_sequence_number',
    'Permit Subtype': 'permit_subtype',
    'Oil Gas': 'oil_gas',
    'Site Fill': 'site_fill',
    'Filing Date': 'filing_date',
    'Issuance Date': 'issuance_date',
    'Expiration Date': 'expiration_date',
    'Job Start Date': 'job_start_date',
    "Permittee's First Name": 'permittee_first_name',
    "Permittee's Last Name": 'permittee_last_name',
    "Permittee's Business Name": 'permittee_business_name',
    "Permittee's Phone #": 'permittee_phone_number',
    "Permittee's License Type": 'permittee_license_type',
    "Permittee's License #": 'permittee_license_number',
    'Act as Superintendent': 'act_as_superintendent',
    "Permittee's Other Title": 'permittee_other_title',
    'HIC License': 'hic_license',
    "Site Safety Mgr's First Name": 'site_safety_mgr_first_name',
    "Site Safety Mgr's Last Name": 'site_safety_mgr_last_name',
    'Site Safety Mgr Business Name': 'site_safety_mgr_business_name',
    'Superintendent First & Last Name': 'superintendent_first_last_name',
    'Superintendent Business Name': 'superintendent_business_name',
    "Owner's Business Type": 'owner_business_type',
    'Non-Profit': 'non_profit',
    "Owner's Business Name": 'owner_business_name',
    "Owner's First Name": 'owner_first_name',
    "Owner's Last Name": 'owner_last_name',
    "Owner's House #": 'owner_house_number',
    "Owner's House Street Name": 'owner_house_street_name',
    "Owner's House City": 'owner_house_city',
    "Owner's House State": 'owner_house_state',
    "Owner's House Zip Code": 'owner_house_zip_code',
    "Owner's Phone #": 'owner_phone_number',
    'DOBRunDate': 'dob_run_date',
    'PERMIT_SI_NO': 'permit_si_no',
    'LATITUDE': 'latitude',
    'LONGITUDE': 'longitude',
    'COUNCIL_DISTRICT': 'council_district',
    'CENSUS_TRACT': 'census_tract',
    'NTA_NAME': 'nta_name'
}

df_mapped = df_latest.rename(columns=column_mapping)
df_mapped = df_mapped.fillna('')
df_mapped['issuance_date'] = df_mapped['issuance_date'].astype(str)

# Save processed file
df_mapped.to_csv('DOB_Permit_Processed.csv', index=False)
print(f"Saved processed file: DOB_Permit_Processed.csv ({len(df_mapped)} rows)")
EOF
```

### QUERY 25 (New Query) - Create Qualified Leads View

```
-- Create comprehensive view to identify qualified property management leads
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW qualified_leads AS
WITH recent_projects AS (
  -- Get recent DOB NOW applications for potential 10+ unit buildings
  SELECT DISTINCT
    app.bin,
    app.borough,
    app.block,
    app.lot,
    app.house_number,
    app.street_name,
    app.zip_code,
    app.job_number,
    app.job_description,
    app.work_type,
    app.applicant_business_name,
    app.applicant_first_name,
    app.applicant_last_name,
    app.filing_date,
    app.total_construction_floor_area,
    app.estimated_job_costs
  FROM dob_now_applications app
  WHERE 
    app.filing_date >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '12 months'
    AND (
      app.job_description ILIKE '%unit%'
      OR app.job_description ILIKE '%apartment%'
      OR app.job_description ILIKE '%dwelling%'
      OR app.job_description ILIKE '%residential%'
      OR app.total_construction_floor_area::numeric > 8000
      OR app.estimated_job_costs::numeric > 300000
    )
),

building_info AS (
  -- Get building registration info for 10+ unit buildings
  SELECT 
    reg.bin,
    reg.house_number,
    reg.street_name,
    reg.borough,
    reg.zip_code,
    reg.block,
    reg.lot,
    reg.num_of_stories,
    reg.units_res::numeric AS residential_units,
    reg.year_built,
    reg.building_class,
    reg.owner_type,
    reg.owner_name,
    reg.owner_house_number,
    reg.owner_house_street,
    reg.owner_house_city,
    reg.owner_house_state,
    reg.owner_house_zip_code
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
  WHERE reg.units_res::numeric >= 10
),

management_status AS (
  -- Check if building has management company
  SELECT 
    reg.bin,
    reg.registration_id,
    COUNT(cont.id) as management_contacts,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont.business_name, ', ') as management_companies,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont.business_phone, ', ') as management_phones
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
  LEFT JOIN registration_contacts cont ON reg.registration_id = cont.registration_id
  WHERE cont.contact_type IN ('CorporateOwner', 'Agent', 'Manager')
  GROUP BY reg.bin, reg.registration_id
),

owner_contact_info AS (
  -- Get latest owner contact info from approved permits
  SELECT DISTINCT
    perm.bin_number as bin,
    perm.owner_business_name,
    perm.owner_first_name,
    perm.owner_last_name,
    perm.owner_phone_number,
    perm.owner_house_city,
    perm.owner_house_state,
    perm.issuance_date,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as rn
  FROM dob_permit_issuance perm
  WHERE perm.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
     OR (perm.owner_first_name IS NOT NULL AND perm.owner_last_name IS NOT NULL)
)

-- Final qualified leads output
SELECT 
  -- Building identification
  COALESCE(proj.bin, bldg.bin) as bin,
  COALESCE(proj.borough, bldg.borough) as borough,
  COALESCE(proj.house_number || ' ' || proj.street_name, 
           bldg.house_number || ' ' || bldg.street_name) as address,
  COALESCE(proj.zip_code, bldg.zip_code) as zip_code,
  
  -- Project info
  proj.job_number,
  proj.job_description,
  proj.work_type,
  proj.filing_date,
  proj.estimated_job_costs,
  
  -- Building info
  bldg.residential_units,
  bldg.num_of_stories,
  bldg.year_built,
  bldg.building_class,
  bldg.owner_name as registered_owner,
  
  -- Owner contact (from permits)
  owner.owner_business_name as permit_owner_business,
  owner.owner_first_name as permit_owner_first,
  owner.owner_last_name as permit_owner_last,
  owner.owner_phone_number as permit_owner_phone,
  owner.owner_house_city as permit_owner_city,
  owner.owner_house_state as permit_owner_state,
  
  -- Management status
  COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) as management_count,
  mgmt.management_companies,
  mgmt.management_phones,
  
  -- Lead qualification score
  CASE 
    WHEN bldg.residential_units >= 100 THEN 10
    WHEN bldg.residential_units >= 50 THEN 8
    WHEN bldg.residential_units >= 25 THEN 6  
    WHEN bldg.residential_units >= 15 THEN 4
    WHEN bldg.residential_units >= 10 THEN 2
    ELSE 0
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.estimated_job_costs::numeric > 2000000 THEN 5
    WHEN proj.estimated_job_costs::numeric > 1000000 THEN 3
    WHEN proj.estimated_job_costs::numeric > 500000 THEN 2
    WHEN proj.estimated_job_costs::numeric > 100000 THEN 1
    ELSE 0
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) = 0 THEN 5  -- No management = highest score
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) = 1 THEN 2
    ELSE 0
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Contact priority
  CASE
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) = 0 AND bldg.residential_units >= 50 THEN 'IMMEDIATE'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) = 0 AND bldg.residential_units >= 25 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) = 0 AND bldg.residential_units >= 15 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) <= 1 AND bldg.residential_units >= 10 THEN 'LOW'
    ELSE 'RESEARCH'
  END as contact_priority,
  
  CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as generated_at

FROM recent_projects proj
FULL OUTER JOIN building_info bldg ON proj.bin = bldg.bin
LEFT JOIN management_status mgmt ON COALESCE(proj.bin, bldg.bin) = mgmt.bin  
LEFT JOIN owner_contact_info owner ON COALESCE(proj.bin, bldg.bin) = owner.bin AND owner.rn = 1

WHERE 
  -- Must have either recent project activity OR be 10+ unit building
  (proj.bin IS NOT NULL OR bldg.residential_units >= 10)
  -- Must have some contact information
  AND (
    owner.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
    OR owner.owner_first_name IS NOT NULL 
    OR bldg.owner_name IS NOT NULL
  )

ORDER BY lead_score DESC, bldg.residential_units DESC;
```

Claude: ## QUERY 25 (Fixed) - Create Qualified Leads View

```
-- Create comprehensive view to identify qualified property management leads
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW qualified_leads AS
WITH recent_projects AS (
  -- Get recent DOB NOW applications for potential 10+ unit buildings
  SELECT DISTINCT
    app."Bin",
    app."Borough",
    app."Block",
    app."Lot", 
    app."House Number",
    app."Street Name",
    app."Zip Code",
    app."Job Number",
    app."Job Description",
    app."Work Type",
    app."Applicant Business Name",
    app."Applicant First Name",
    app."Applicant Last Name",
    app."Filing Date",
    app."Total Construction Floor Area",
    app."Estimated Job Costs"
  FROM dob_now_applications app
  WHERE 
    app."Filing Date" >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '12 months'
    AND (
      app."Job Description" ILIKE '%unit%'
      OR app."Job Description" ILIKE '%apartment%'
      OR app."Job Description" ILIKE '%dwelling%'
      OR app."Job Description" ILIKE '%residential%'
      OR app."Total Construction Floor Area"::numeric > 8000
      OR app."Estimated Job Costs"::numeric > 300000
    )
),

building_info AS (
  -- Get building registration info for 10+ unit buildings
  SELECT 
    reg.bin,
    reg.house_number,
    reg.street_name,
    reg.borough,
    reg.zip_code,
    reg.block,
    reg.lot,
    reg.num_of_stories,
    reg.units_res::numeric AS residential_units,
    reg.year_built,
    reg.building_class,
    reg.owner_type,
    reg.owner_name,
    reg.owner_house_number,
    reg.owner_house_street,
    reg.owner_house_city,
    reg.owner_house_state,
    reg.owner_house_zip_code
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
  WHERE reg.units_res::numeric >= 10
),

management_status AS (
  -- Check if building has management company
  SELECT 
    reg.bin,
    reg.registration_id,
    COUNT(cont.id) as management_contacts,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont.business_name, ', ') as management_companies,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont.business_phone, ', ') as management_phones
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
  LEFT JOIN registration_contacts cont ON reg.registration_id = cont.registration_id
  WHERE cont.contact_type IN ('CorporateOwner', 'Agent', 'Manager')
  GROUP BY reg.bin, reg.registration_id
),

owner_contact_info AS (
  -- Get latest owner contact info from approved permits
  SELECT DISTINCT
    perm.bin_number as bin,
    perm.owner_business_name,
    perm.owner_first_name,
    perm.owner_last_name,
    perm.owner_phone_number,
    perm.owner_house_city,
    perm.owner_house_state,
    perm.issuance_date,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as rn
  FROM dob_permit_issuance perm
  WHERE perm.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
     OR (perm.owner_first_name IS NOT NULL AND perm.owner_last_name IS NOT NULL)
)

-- Final qualified leads output
SELECT 
  -- Building identification
  COALESCE(proj."Bin", bldg.bin) as bin,
  COALESCE(proj."Borough", bldg.borough) as borough,
  COALESCE(proj."House Number" || ' ' || proj."Street Name", 
           bldg.house_number || ' ' || bldg.street_name) as address,
  COALESCE(proj."Zip Code", bldg.zip_code) as zip_code,
  
  -- Project info
  proj."Job Number" as job_number,
  proj."Job Description" as job_description,
  proj."Work Type" as work_type,
  proj."Filing Date" as filing_date,
  proj."Estimated Job Costs" as estimated_job_costs,
  
  -- Building info
  bldg.residential_units,
  bldg.num_of_stories,
  bldg.year_built,
  bldg.building_class,
  bldg.owner_name as registered_owner,
  
  -- Owner contact (from permits)
  owner.owner_business_name as permit_owner_business,
  owner.owner_first_name as permit_owner_first,
  owner.owner_last_name as permit_owner_last,
  owner.owner_phone_number as permit_owner_phone,
  owner.owner_house_city as permit_owner_city,
  owner.owner_house_state as permit_owner_state,
  
  -- Management status
  COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) as management_count,
  mgmt.management_companies,
  mgmt.management_phones,
  
  -- Lead qualification score
  CASE 
    WHEN bldg.residential_units >= 100 THEN 10
    WHEN bldg.residential_units >= 50 THEN 8
    WHEN bldg.residential_units >= 25 THEN 6  
    WHEN bldg.residential_units >= 15 THEN 4
    WHEN bldg.residential_units >= 10 THEN 2
    ELSE 0
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN proj."Estimated Job Costs"::numeric > 2000000 THEN 5
    WHEN proj."Estimated Job Costs"::numeric > 1000000 THEN 3
    WHEN proj."Estimated Job Costs"::numeric > 500000 THEN 2
    WHEN proj."Estimated Job Costs"::numeric > 100000 THEN 1
    ELSE 0
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) = 0 THEN 5  -- No management = highest score
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) = 1 THEN 2
    ELSE 0
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Contact priority
  CASE
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) = 0 AND bldg.residential_units >= 50 THEN 'IMMEDIATE'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) = 0 AND bldg.residential_units >= 25 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) = 0 AND bldg.residential_units >= 15 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_contacts, 0) <= 1 AND bldg.residential_units >= 10 THEN 'LOW'
    ELSE 'RESEARCH'
  END as contact_priority,
  
  CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as generated_at

FROM recent_projects proj
FULL OUTER JOIN building_info bldg ON proj."Bin" = bldg.bin
LEFT JOIN management_status mgmt ON COALESCE(proj."Bin", bldg.bin) = mgmt.bin  
LEFT JOIN owner_contact_info owner ON COALESCE(proj."Bin", bldg.bin) = owner.bin AND owner.rn = 1

WHERE 
  -- Must have either recent project activity OR be 10+ unit building
  (proj."Bin" IS NOT NULL OR bldg.residential_units >= 10)
  -- Must have some contact information
  AND (
    owner.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
    OR owner.owner_first_name IS NOT NULL 
    OR bldg.owner_name IS NOT NULL
  )

ORDER BY lead_score DESC, bldg.residential_units DESC;
```

-- Check all table column names
SELECT 
    table_name,
    column_name,
    ordinal_position
FROM information_schema.columns 
WHERE table_name IN (
    'permit_applications',
    'dob_now_applications', 
    'dob_now_approved_permits',
    'dob_permit_issuance',
    'multiple_dwelling_registrations',
    'registration_contacts'
)
ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position;

- Registration Contacts has: Type (Individual Owner, Corporate Owner, Managing Agent), CorporationName, etc.

Claude: ## QUERY 27 (New Query) - Qualified Leads with Correct Column Names

```
-- Create comprehensive view to identify qualified property management leads
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW qualified_leads AS
WITH recent_dob_now_projects AS (
  -- Get recent DOB NOW applications for potential 10+ unit buildings
  SELECT DISTINCT
    app."Bin" as bin,
    app."Borough" as borough,
    app."Block" as block,
    app."LOT" as lot,
    app."House No" as house_number,
    app."Street Name" as street_name,
    app."Postcode" as zip_code,
    app."Job Filing Number" as job_number,
    app."Job Type" as job_type,
    app."Initial Cost" as initial_cost,
    app."Total Construction Floor Area" as total_construction_floor_area,
    app."Existing Dwelling Units" as existing_dwelling_units,
    app."Proposed Dwelling Units" as proposed_dwelling_units,
    app."Owner's Business Name" as owner_business_name,
    app."Filing Date" as filing_date
  FROM dob_now_applications app
  WHERE 
    app."Filing Date" >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '12 months'
    AND (
      -- Look for multi-unit residential projects
      app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::numeric >= 10
      OR app."Existing Dwelling Units"::numeric >= 10
      OR app."Total Construction Floor Area"::numeric > 8000
      OR app."Initial Cost"::numeric > 300000
      OR app."Job Type" ILIKE '%residential%'
      OR app."Job Type" ILIKE '%dwelling%'
    )
),

approved_permits_with_owners AS (
  -- Get owner info from approved permits
  SELECT DISTINCT
    perm."Bin" as bin,
    perm."Owner Business Name" as owner_business_name,
    perm."Owner Name" as owner_name,
    perm."Owner Street Address" as owner_address,
    perm."Owner City" as owner_city,
    perm."Owner State" as owner_state,
    perm."Owner Zip Code" as owner_zip,
    perm."Estimated Job Costs" as estimated_job_costs,
    perm."Job Description" as job_description,
    perm."Approved Date" as approved_date,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY perm."Bin" ORDER BY perm."Approved Date" DESC) as rn
  FROM dob_now_approved_permits perm
  WHERE perm."Owner Business Name" IS NOT NULL 
     OR perm."Owner Name" IS NOT NULL
),

issued_permits_with_owners AS (
  -- Get owner info from issued permits (your imported data)
  SELECT DISTINCT
    perm.bin_number as bin,
    perm.owner_business_name,
    perm.owner_first_name,
    perm.owner_last_name,
    perm.owner_house_city,
    perm.owner_house_state,
    perm.owner_house_zip_code,
    perm.owner_phone_number,
    perm.issuance_date,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as rn
  FROM dob_permit_issuance perm
  WHERE perm.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
     OR (perm.owner_first_name IS NOT NULL AND perm.owner_last_name IS NOT NULL)
),

building_registrations AS (
  -- Get building registration info
  SELECT 
    reg."BIN" as bin,
    reg."HouseNumber" as house_number,
    reg."StreetName" as street_name,
    reg."Boro" as borough,
    reg."Zip" as zip_code,
    reg."Block" as block,
    reg."Lot" as lot,
    reg."RegistrationID" as registration_id,
    reg."LastRegistrationDate" as last_registration_date
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
),

management_contacts AS (
  -- Check for management companies
  SELECT 
    reg."BIN" as bin,
    COUNT(cont.id) as management_count,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont."CorporationName", ', ') as management_companies,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont."Type", ', ') as contact_types
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
  LEFT JOIN registration_contacts cont ON reg."RegistrationID" = cont."RegistrationID"
  WHERE cont."Type" IN ('CorporateOwner', 'Managing Agent', 'Head Officer')
  GROUP BY reg."BIN"
)

-- Final qualified leads output
SELECT 
  -- Building identification
  COALESCE(proj.bin, approved.bin, issued.bin, bldg.bin) as bin,
  COALESCE(proj.borough, bldg.borough) as borough,
  COALESCE(proj.house_number || ' ' || proj.street_name, 
           bldg.house_number || ' ' || bldg.street_name) as address,
  COALESCE(proj.zip_code, bldg.zip_code) as zip_code,
  
  -- Project info
  proj.job_number,
  proj.job_type,
  proj.filing_date,
  proj.initial_cost,
  proj.total_construction_floor_area,
  proj.existing_dwelling_units,
  proj.proposed_dwelling_units,
  
  -- Building registration info  
  bldg.registration_id,
  bldg.last_registration_date,
  
  -- Owner information (prioritizing most recent)
  COALESCE(
    proj.owner_business_name,
    approved.owner_business_name, 
    issued.owner_business_name
  ) as owner_business_name,
  
  COALESCE(
    approved.owner_name,
    issued.owner_first_name || ' ' || issued.owner_last_name
  ) as owner_name,
  
  COALESCE(
    approved.owner_address,
    issued.owner_house_city || ', ' || issued.owner_house_state
  ) as owner_address,
  
  COALESCE(approved.owner_city, issued.owner_house_city) as owner_city,
  COALESCE(approved.owner_state, issued.owner_house_state) as owner_state,
  COALESCE(approved.owner_zip, issued.owner_house_zip_code) as owner_zip,
  issued.owner_phone_number,
  
  -- Management status
  COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) as management_count,
  mgmt.management_companies,
  mgmt.contact_types,
  
  -- Lead qualification score
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric >= 100 THEN 10
    WHEN proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric >= 50 THEN 8
    WHEN proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric >= 25 THEN 6  
    WHEN proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric >= 15 THEN 4
    WHEN proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric >= 10 THEN 3
    WHEN proj.existing_dwelling_units::numeric >= 10 THEN 2
    ELSE 1
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.initial_cost::numeric > 2000000 THEN 5
    WHEN proj.initial_cost::numeric > 1000000 THEN 3
    WHEN proj.initial_cost::numeric > 500000 THEN 2
    WHEN proj.initial_cost::numeric > 100000 THEN 1
    ELSE 0
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 5  -- No management = highest score
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 1 THEN 2
    ELSE 0
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Contact priority
  CASE
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 AND proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric >= 50 THEN 'IMMEDIATE'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 AND proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric >= 25 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 AND proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric >= 15 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) <= 1 AND proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric >= 10 THEN 'LOW'
    ELSE 'RESEARCH'
  END as contact_priority,
  
  CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as generated_at

FROM recent_dob_now_projects proj
LEFT JOIN approved_permits_with_owners approved ON proj.bin = approved.bin AND approved.rn = 1
LEFT JOIN issued_permits_with_owners issued ON proj.bin = issued.bin AND issued.rn = 1
LEFT JOIN building_registrations bldg ON proj.bin = bldg.bin
LEFT JOIN management_contacts mgmt ON proj.bin = mgmt.bin

WHERE 
  -- Must have some contact information
  (
    proj.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
    OR approved.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL
    OR approved.owner_name IS NOT NULL
    OR issued.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
    OR issued.owner_first_name IS NOT NULL
  )
  -- Focus on 10+ unit projects
  AND (
    proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric >= 10
    OR proj.existing_dwelling_units::numeric >= 10
    OR proj.total_construction_floor_area::numeric > 8000
  )

ORDER BY lead_score DESC, proj.proposed_dwelling_units::numeric DESC;
```

Claude: ## QUERY 27 (Fixed) - Qualified Leads with Correct Data Types

```
-- Create comprehensive view to identify qualified property management leads
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW qualified_leads AS
WITH recent_dob_now_projects AS (
  -- Get recent DOB NOW applications for potential 10+ unit buildings
  SELECT DISTINCT
    app."Bin" as bin,
    app."Borough" as borough,
    app."Block" as block,
    app."LOT" as lot,
    app."House No" as house_number,
    app."Street Name" as street_name,
    app."Postcode" as zip_code,
    app."Job Filing Number" as job_number,
    app."Job Type" as job_type,
    app."Initial Cost" as initial_cost,
    app."Total Construction Floor Area" as total_construction_floor_area,
    app."Existing Dwelling Units" as existing_dwelling_units,
    app."Proposed Dwelling Units" as proposed_dwelling_units,
    app."Owner's Business Name" as owner_business_name,
    app."Filing Date" as filing_date
  FROM dob_now_applications app
  WHERE 
    -- Convert text date to proper date type for comparison
    app."Filing Date"::date >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '12 months'
    AND (
      -- Look for multi-unit residential projects
      COALESCE(app."Proposed Dwelling Units", '0')::numeric >= 10
      OR COALESCE(app."Existing Dwelling Units", '0')::numeric >= 10
      OR COALESCE(app."Total Construction Floor Area", '0')::numeric > 8000
      OR COALESCE(app."Initial Cost", '0')::numeric > 300000
      OR app."Job Type" ILIKE '%residential%'
      OR app."Job Type" ILIKE '%dwelling%'
    )
),

approved_permits_with_owners AS (
  -- Get owner info from approved permits
  SELECT DISTINCT
    perm."Bin" as bin,
    perm."Owner Business Name" as owner_business_name,
    perm."Owner Name" as owner_name,
    perm."Owner Street Address" as owner_address,
    perm."Owner City" as owner_city,
    perm."Owner State" as owner_state,
    perm."Owner Zip Code" as owner_zip,
    perm."Estimated Job Costs" as estimated_job_costs,
    perm."Job Description" as job_description,
    perm."Approved Date" as approved_date,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY perm."Bin" ORDER BY perm."Approved Date"::date DESC NULLS LAST) as rn
  FROM dob_now_approved_permits perm
  WHERE perm."Owner Business Name" IS NOT NULL 
     OR perm."Owner Name" IS NOT NULL
),

issued_permits_with_owners AS (
  -- Get owner info from issued permits (your imported data)
  SELECT DISTINCT
    perm.bin_number as bin,
    perm.owner_business_name,
    perm.owner_first_name,
    perm.owner_last_name,
    perm.owner_house_city,
    perm.owner_house_state,
    perm.owner_house_zip_code,
    perm.owner_phone_number,
    perm.issuance_date,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date::date DESC NULLS LAST) as rn
  FROM dob_permit_issuance perm
  WHERE perm.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
     OR (perm.owner_first_name IS NOT NULL AND perm.owner_last_name IS NOT NULL)
),

building_registrations AS (
  -- Get building registration info
  SELECT 
    reg."BIN" as bin,
    reg."HouseNumber" as house_number,
    reg."StreetName" as street_name,
    reg."Boro" as borough,
    reg."Zip" as zip_code,
    reg."Block" as block,
    reg."Lot" as lot,
    reg."RegistrationID" as registration_id,
    reg."LastRegistrationDate" as last_registration_date
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
),

management_contacts AS (
  -- Check for management companies
  SELECT 
    reg."BIN" as bin,
    COUNT(cont.id) as management_count,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont."CorporationName", ', ') as management_companies,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont."Type", ', ') as contact_types
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
  LEFT JOIN registration_contacts cont ON reg."RegistrationID" = cont."RegistrationID"
  WHERE cont."Type" IN ('Corporate Owner', 'Managing Agent', 'Head Officer')
  GROUP BY reg."BIN"
)

-- Final qualified leads output
SELECT 
  -- Building identification
  COALESCE(proj.bin, approved.bin, issued.bin, bldg.bin) as bin,
  COALESCE(proj.borough, bldg.borough) as borough,
  COALESCE(proj.house_number || ' ' || proj.street_name, 
           bldg.house_number || ' ' || bldg.street_name) as address,
  COALESCE(proj.zip_code, bldg.zip_code) as zip_code,
  
  -- Project info
  proj.job_number,
  proj.job_type,
  proj.filing_date,
  proj.initial_cost,
  proj.total_construction_floor_area,
  proj.existing_dwelling_units,
  proj.proposed_dwelling_units,
  
  -- Building registration info  
  bldg.registration_id,
  bldg.last_registration_date,
  
  -- Owner information (prioritizing most recent)
  COALESCE(
    proj.owner_business_name,
    approved.owner_business_name, 
    issued.owner_business_name
  ) as owner_business_name,
  
  COALESCE(
    approved.owner_name,
    CASE 
      WHEN issued.owner_first_name IS NOT NULL AND issued.owner_last_name IS NOT NULL 
      THEN issued.owner_first_name || ' ' || issued.owner_last_name
      ELSE NULL
    END
  ) as owner_name,
  
  COALESCE(
    approved.owner_address,
    CASE 
      WHEN issued.owner_house_city IS NOT NULL AND issued.owner_house_state IS NOT NULL 
      THEN issued.owner_house_city || ', ' || issued.owner_house_state
      ELSE NULL
    END
  ) as owner_address,
  
  COALESCE(approved.owner_city, issued.owner_house_city) as owner_city,
  COALESCE(approved.owner_state, issued.owner_house_state) as owner_state,
  COALESCE(approved.owner_zip, issued.owner_house_zip_code) as owner_zip,
  issued.owner_phone_number,
  
  -- Management status
  COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) as management_count,
  mgmt.management_companies,
  mgmt.contact_types,
  
  -- Lead qualification score
  CASE 
    WHEN COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 100 THEN 10
    WHEN COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 50 THEN 8
    WHEN COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 25 THEN 6  
    WHEN COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 15 THEN 4
    WHEN COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 10 THEN 3
    WHEN COALESCE(proj.existing_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 10 THEN 2
    ELSE 1
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN COALESCE(proj.initial_cost, '0')::numeric > 2000000 THEN 5
    WHEN COALESCE(proj.initial_cost, '0')::numeric > 1000000 THEN 3
    WHEN COALESCE(proj.initial_cost, '0')::numeric > 500000 THEN 2
    WHEN COALESCE(proj.initial_cost, '0')::numeric > 100000 THEN 1
    ELSE 0
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 5  -- No management = highest score
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 1 THEN 2
    ELSE 0
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Contact priority
  CASE
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 AND COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 50 THEN 'IMMEDIATE'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 AND COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 25 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 AND COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 15 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) <= 1 AND COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 10 THEN 'LOW'
    ELSE 'RESEARCH'
  END as contact_priority,
  
  CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as generated_at

FROM recent_dob_now_projects proj
LEFT JOIN approved_permits_with_owners approved ON proj.bin = approved.bin AND approved.rn = 1
LEFT JOIN issued_permits_with_owners issued ON proj.bin = issued.bin AND issued.rn = 1
LEFT JOIN building_registrations bldg ON proj.bin = bldg.bin
LEFT JOIN management_contacts mgmt ON proj.bin = mgmt.bin

WHERE 
  -- Must have some contact information
  (
    proj.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
    OR approved.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL
    OR approved.owner_name IS NOT NULL
    OR issued.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
    OR issued.owner_first_name IS NOT NULL
  )
  -- Focus on 10+ unit projects
  AND (
    COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 10
    OR COALESCE(proj.existing_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric >= 10
    OR COALESCE(proj.total_construction_floor_area, '0')::numeric > 8000
  )

ORDER BY lead_score DESC, COALESCE(proj.proposed_dwelling_units, '0')::numeric DESC;
```

- ✅ Added ::date casting for date comparisons
- ✅ Added COALESCE to handle NULL values in numeric conversions
- ✅ Fixed NULL handling in string concatenations
- ✅ Added NULLS LAST in ORDER BY clauses

`::date`

`COALESCE`

`NULLS LAST`

Claude: ## QUERY 33 (New Query) - Working Qualified Leads View

```
-- Create working qualified leads view with proper date handling
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW qualified_leads AS
WITH recent_projects AS (
  -- Get recent projects with dwelling units or high value
  SELECT 
    app."Bin" as bin,
    app."Borough" as borough,
    app."House No" as house_number,
    app."Street Name" as street_name,
    app."Postcode" as zip_code,
    app."Job Filing Number" as job_number,
    app."Job Type" as job_type,
    app."Filing Date" as filing_date,
    COALESCE(app."Proposed Dwelling Units", '0') as proposed_units_text,
    COALESCE(app."Existing Dwelling Units", '0') as existing_units_text,
    COALESCE(app."Initial Cost", '0') as initial_cost_text,
    app."Owner's Business Name" as owner_business_name
  FROM dob_now_applications app
  WHERE 
    -- Filter for recent projects (last 18 months)
    app."Filing Date"::timestamp >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '18 months'
    AND app."Owner's Business Name" IS NOT NULL
    AND app."Owner's Business Name" NOT IN ('SELF', 'PR', '')
    AND (
      -- Look for residential projects with units or high value
      (app."Proposed Dwelling Units" IS NOT NULL AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units" != '' AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units" != '0')
      OR (app."Existing Dwelling Units" IS NOT NULL AND app."Existing Dwelling Units" != '' AND app."Existing Dwelling Units" != '0')
      OR (app."Initial Cost" IS NOT NULL AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric > 200000)
      OR app."Job Type" ILIKE '%residential%'
      OR app."Job Type" ILIKE '%dwelling%'
      OR app."Job Type" ILIKE '%apartment%'
    )
),

owner_contacts AS (
  -- Get owner contact info from permit issuance data
  SELECT DISTINCT
    perm.bin_number as bin,
    perm.owner_business_name,
    perm.owner_first_name,
    perm.owner_last_name,
    perm.owner_phone_number,
    perm.owner_house_city,
    perm.owner_house_state,
    perm.issuance_date,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as rn
  FROM dob_permit_issuance perm
  WHERE perm.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
     OR (perm.owner_first_name IS NOT NULL AND perm.owner_last_name IS NOT NULL)
),

management_check AS (
  -- Check for existing management companies
  SELECT 
    reg."BIN" as bin,
    COUNT(cont.id) as management_count,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont."CorporationName", ', ') as management_companies
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
  LEFT JOIN registration_contacts cont ON reg."RegistrationID" = cont."RegistrationID"
  WHERE cont."Type" IN ('Corporate Owner', 'Managing Agent')
    AND cont."CorporationName" IS NOT NULL 
    AND cont."CorporationName" != ''
  GROUP BY reg."BIN"
)

-- Final qualified leads
SELECT 
  proj.bin,
  proj.borough,
  proj.house_number || ' ' || proj.street_name as address,
  proj.zip_code,
  proj.job_number,
  proj.job_type,
  proj.filing_date,
  
  -- Unit counts (converted to numeric where possible)
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN proj.proposed_units_text::integer
    ELSE 0
  END as proposed_units,
  
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.existing_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN proj.existing_units_text::integer
    ELSE 0
  END as existing_units,
  
  -- Project cost
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' THEN proj.initial_cost_text::numeric
    ELSE 0
  END as initial_cost,
  
  -- Owner information
  proj.owner_business_name as project_owner,
  COALESCE(
    owner.owner_business_name,
    owner.owner_first_name || ' ' || owner.owner_last_name
  ) as owner_contact,
  owner.owner_phone_number,
  owner.owner_house_city,
  owner.owner_house_state,
  
  -- Management status
  COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) as existing_management_count,
  mgmt.management_companies,
  
  -- Lead priority score
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 50 THEN 10
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 25 THEN 8
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 10 THEN 6
    WHEN proj.existing_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.existing_units_text::integer >= 10 THEN 4
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND proj.initial_cost_text::numeric > 1000000 THEN 5
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND proj.initial_cost_text::numeric > 500000 THEN 3
    ELSE 1
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 5  -- No existing management
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 1 THEN 2
    ELSE 0
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Contact priority
  CASE
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 'HIGH_PRIORITY'
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) <= 1 THEN 'MEDIUM_PRIORITY'
    ELSE 'LOW_PRIORITY'
  END as contact_priority,
  
  CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as generated_at

FROM recent_projects proj
LEFT JOIN owner_contacts owner ON proj.bin = owner.bin AND owner.rn = 1
LEFT JOIN management_check mgmt ON proj.bin = mgmt.bin

ORDER BY lead_score DESC, 
         CASE WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN proj.proposed_units_text::integer ELSE 0 END DESC;
```

- ✅ Handles the MM/DD/YYYY date format properly
- ✅ Filters out "SELF" and "PR" (not real businesses)
- ✅ Looks for projects with units or high value
- ✅ Cross-references with your owner contact data
- ✅ Checks for existing management companies
- ✅ Scores leads by priority

Human: Success. No rows returned

Claude: ## QUERY 37 (New Query) - Create Targeted Qualified Leads

```
-- Create targeted qualified leads view for multi-unit residential projects
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW qualified_leads AS
WITH high_value_projects AS (
  -- Focus on New Building projects with multiple units or high value
  SELECT 
    app."Bin" as bin,
    app."Borough" as borough,
    app."House No" as house_number,
    app."Street Name" as street_name,
    app."Postcode" as zip_code,
    app."Job Filing Number" as job_number,
    app."Job Type" as job_type,
    app."Filing Date" as filing_date,
    app."Proposed Dwelling Units" as proposed_units_text,
    app."Initial Cost" as initial_cost_text,
    app."Owner's Business Name" as owner_business_name
  FROM dob_now_applications app
  WHERE 
    app."Job Type" = 'New Building'
    AND app."Owner's Business Name" IS NOT NULL
    AND app."Owner's Business Name" NOT IN ('SELF', 'PR', '')
    AND (
      -- Multi-unit residential (10+ units) OR high value projects
      (app."Proposed Dwelling Units" IS NOT NULL 
       AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
       AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 10)
      OR 
      (app."Initial Cost" IS NOT NULL 
       AND app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' 
       AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric >= 1000000)
    )
),

owner_contacts AS (
  -- Get additional owner contact info from permit issuance
  SELECT DISTINCT
    perm.bin_number as bin,
    perm.owner_business_name as permit_owner_business,
    perm.owner_first_name,
    perm.owner_last_name,
    perm.owner_phone_number,
    perm.owner_house_city,
    perm.owner_house_state,
    perm.owner_house_zip_code,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as rn
  FROM dob_permit_issuance perm
  WHERE perm.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
),

management_status AS (
  -- Check if building already has management
  SELECT 
    reg."BIN" as bin,
    COUNT(cont.id) as management_count,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont."CorporationName", ', ') as existing_management
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
  LEFT JOIN registration_contacts cont ON reg."RegistrationID" = cont."RegistrationID"
  WHERE cont."Type" IN ('Corporate Owner', 'Managing Agent')
    AND cont."CorporationName" IS NOT NULL 
    AND cont."CorporationName" != ''
  GROUP BY reg."BIN"
)

-- Final qualified leads with scoring
SELECT 
  proj.bin,
  proj.borough,
  proj.house_number || ' ' || proj.street_name as address,
  proj.zip_code,
  proj.job_number,
  proj.filing_date,
  
  -- Project details
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN proj.proposed_units_text::integer
    ELSE 0
  END as proposed_dwelling_units,
  
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' THEN proj.initial_cost_text::numeric
    ELSE 0
  END as project_value,
  
  -- Owner information
  proj.owner_business_name as primary_owner,
  COALESCE(owner.permit_owner_business, proj.owner_business_name) as contact_business,
  owner.owner_phone_number,
  owner.owner_house_city || CASE WHEN owner.owner_house_state IS NOT NULL THEN ', ' || owner.owner_house_state ELSE '' END as owner_location,
  
  -- Management status
  COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) as existing_management_count,
  mgmt.existing_management,
  
  -- Lead scoring (higher = better prospect)
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 100 THEN 20
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 50 THEN 15
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 25 THEN 12
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 15 THEN 8
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 10 THEN 5
    ELSE 0
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND proj.initial_cost_text::numeric >= 5000000 THEN 10
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND proj.initial_cost_text::numeric >= 2000000 THEN 5
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND proj.initial_cost_text::numeric >= 1000000 THEN 3
    ELSE 0
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 10  -- No existing management = highest priority
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 1 THEN 3
    ELSE 0
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Contact priority classification
  CASE
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 50 
         AND COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 'IMMEDIATE'
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 25 
         AND COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) <= 1 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 15 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 10 THEN 'QUALIFIED'
    ELSE 'RESEARCH'
  END as contact_priority,
  
  CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as generated_at

FROM high_value_projects proj
LEFT JOIN owner_contacts owner ON proj.bin = owner.bin AND owner.rn = 1
LEFT JOIN management_status mgmt ON proj.bin = mgmt.bin

ORDER BY lead_score DESC, 
         CASE WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN proj.proposed_units_text::integer ELSE 0 END DESC;
```

- ✅ TOT DEVELOPERS (213 units)
- ✅ ASCO GROUP LLC (60 units, $1M project)

Claude: ## QUERY 37 (Fixed) - Drop and Recreate View

```
-- Drop existing view and recreate
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS qualified_leads;

-- Create targeted qualified leads view for multi-unit residential projects
CREATE VIEW qualified_leads AS
WITH high_value_projects AS (
  -- Focus on New Building projects with multiple units or high value
  SELECT 
    app."Bin" as bin,
    app."Borough" as borough,
    app."House No" as house_number,
    app."Street Name" as street_name,
    app."Postcode" as zip_code,
    app."Job Filing Number" as job_number,
    app."Job Type" as job_type,
    app."Filing Date" as filing_date,
    app."Proposed Dwelling Units" as proposed_units_text,
    app."Initial Cost" as initial_cost_text,
    app."Owner's Business Name" as owner_business_name
  FROM dob_now_applications app
  WHERE 
    app."Job Type" = 'New Building'
    AND app."Owner's Business Name" IS NOT NULL
    AND app."Owner's Business Name" NOT IN ('SELF', 'PR', '')
    AND (
      -- Multi-unit residential (10+ units) OR high value projects
      (app."Proposed Dwelling Units" IS NOT NULL 
       AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
       AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 10)
      OR 
      (app."Initial Cost" IS NOT NULL 
       AND app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' 
       AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric >= 1000000)
    )
),

owner_contacts AS (
  -- Get additional owner contact info from permit issuance
  SELECT DISTINCT
    perm.bin_number as bin,
    perm.owner_business_name as permit_owner_business,
    perm.owner_first_name,
    perm.owner_last_name,
    perm.owner_phone_number,
    perm.owner_house_city,
    perm.owner_house_state,
    perm.owner_house_zip_code,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as rn
  FROM dob_permit_issuance perm
  WHERE perm.owner_business_name IS NOT NULL 
),

management_status AS (
  -- Check if building already has management
  SELECT 
    reg."BIN" as bin,
    COUNT(cont.id) as management_count,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont."CorporationName", ', ') as existing_management
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
  LEFT JOIN registration_contacts cont ON reg."RegistrationID" = cont."RegistrationID"
  WHERE cont."Type" IN ('Corporate Owner', 'Managing Agent')
    AND cont."CorporationName" IS NOT NULL 
    AND cont."CorporationName" != ''
  GROUP BY reg."BIN"
)

-- Final qualified leads with scoring
SELECT 
  proj.bin,
  proj.borough,
  proj.house_number || ' ' || proj.street_name as address,
  proj.zip_code,
  proj.job_number,
  proj.filing_date,
  
  -- Project details
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN proj.proposed_units_text::integer
    ELSE 0
  END as proposed_dwelling_units,
  
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' THEN proj.initial_cost_text::numeric
    ELSE 0
  END as project_value,
  
  -- Owner information
  proj.owner_business_name as primary_owner,
  COALESCE(owner.permit_owner_business, proj.owner_business_name) as contact_business,
  owner.owner_phone_number,
  owner.owner_house_city || CASE WHEN owner.owner_house_state IS NOT NULL THEN ', ' || owner.owner_house_state ELSE '' END as owner_location,
  
  -- Management status
  COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) as existing_management_count,
  mgmt.existing_management,
  
  -- Lead scoring (higher = better prospect)
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 100 THEN 20
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 50 THEN 15
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 25 THEN 12
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 15 THEN 8
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 10 THEN 5
    ELSE 0
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND proj.initial_cost_text::numeric >= 5000000 THEN 10
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND proj.initial_cost_text::numeric >= 2000000 THEN 5
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND proj.initial_cost_text::numeric >= 1000000 THEN 3
    ELSE 0
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 10  -- No existing management = highest priority
    WHEN COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 1 THEN 3
    ELSE 0
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Contact priority classification
  CASE
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 50 
         AND COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 'IMMEDIATE'
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 25 
         AND COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) <= 1 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 15 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 10 THEN 'QUALIFIED'
    ELSE 'RESEARCH'
  END as contact_priority,
  
  CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as generated_at

FROM high_value_projects proj
LEFT JOIN owner_contacts owner ON proj.bin = owner.bin AND owner.rn = 1
LEFT JOIN management_status mgmt ON proj.bin = mgmt.bin

ORDER BY lead_score DESC, 
         CASE WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN proj.proposed_units_text::integer ELSE 0 END DESC;

Claude: ## Current Lead Qualification Framework

### Step 1: Building/Project Qualification

```
New Building Projects → 10+ Units OR $1M+ Value → Recent Activity (18 months)
```

### Step 2: Owner/Developer Classification

```
Primary Owner → Cross-reference with Permit Data → Contact Information → Management Status
```

### Step 3: Lead Scoring Matrix

```
Unit Count: 100+ units = 20 pts, 50+ = 15 pts, 25+ = 12 pts, 15+ = 8 pts, 10+ = 5 pts
Project Value: $5M+ = 10 pts, $2M+ = 5 pts, $1M+ = 3 pts  
Management Status: No Management = 10 pts, Limited = 3 pts, Full Management = 0 pts
```

## Questions for Customization:

### 1. Self-Managing Developer Strategy

- Should we include self-managing developers as prospects? (They might want to outsource)
- What size threshold makes self-managing developers worth pursuing? (50+ units? 100+ units?)
- Any specific developer names you know are good/bad prospects?

### 2. Project Type Preferences

- Focus only on residential? Or include mixed-use commercial?
- Minimum unit count you want to pursue? (Currently 10+)
- Minimum project value threshold? (Currently $1M+)

### 3. Geographic Targeting

- Any boroughs you want to prioritize/exclude?
- Should we weight Manhattan projects higher?

### 4. Management Company Exclusions

- Any management companies that indicate "no opportunity"?
- Should we target buildings with 1 management contact as "changeable"?

### 5. Developer Quality Indicators

- Known high-quality developers you definitely want to target?
- Any red flags in company names that indicate low-quality prospects?

## Proposed Customizable Framework:

```
-- Flexible scoring system you can adjust
CASE 
  -- High-value self-managing developers (might outsource)
  WHEN developer_name IN ('NAFTALI GROUP', 'EXTELL', 'RELATED') 
       AND units >= 50 AND no_management THEN 25  -- Highest priority
       
  -- Medium developers with growth potential  
  WHEN units >= 25 AND no_management THEN 15
  
  -- Smaller developers likely to need help
  WHEN units >= 10 AND no_management THEN 10
  
  -- Buildings with changeable management
  WHEN management_count = 1 AND units >= 25 THEN 8
  
  -- Exclude certain patterns
  WHEN developer_name LIKE '%LLC%' AND units < 15 THEN 0  -- Skip small LLCs
  
END as lead_score
```

Claude: ## Phase 1: Complete the Database Foundation

### QUERY 38 - Enhanced Qualified Leads with CRM-Ready Fields

```
-- Enhanced qualified leads with CRM-ready structure
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW qualified_leads_crm AS
WITH project_data AS (
  SELECT 
    app."Bin" as bin,
    app."Borough" as borough,
    app."House No" || ' ' || app."Street Name" as full_address,
    app."House No" as house_number,
    app."Street Name" as street_name,
    app."Postcode" as zip_code,
    app."Job Filing Number" as job_number,
    app."Job Type" as job_type,
    app."Filing Date" as filing_date,
    app."Proposed Dwelling Units" as proposed_units_text,
    app."Initial Cost" as initial_cost_text,
    app."Owner's Business Name" as primary_owner
  FROM dob_now_applications app
  WHERE app."Job Type" = 'New Building'
    AND app."Owner's Business Name" IS NOT NULL
    AND app."Owner's Business Name" NOT IN ('SELF', 'PR', '')
    AND (
      (app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 10)
      OR (app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric >= 500000)
    )
),

most_recent_permits AS (
  -- Get most recent permit for each BIN
  SELECT DISTINCT
    perm.bin_number as bin,
    FIRST_VALUE(perm.permit_type) OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as latest_permit_type,
    FIRST_VALUE(perm.issuance_date) OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as latest_permit_date,
    FIRST_VALUE(perm.owner_business_name) OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as permit_owner_business,
    FIRST_VALUE(perm.owner_phone_number) OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as owner_phone,
    FIRST_VALUE(perm.owner_house_city || COALESCE(', ' || perm.owner_house_state, '')) OVER (PARTITION BY perm.bin_number ORDER BY perm.issuance_date DESC) as owner_location
  FROM dob_permit_issuance perm
),

current_management AS (
  -- Get current management companies
  SELECT 
    reg."BIN" as bin,
    COUNT(cont.id) as management_count,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont."CorporationName", '; ') as current_managers,
    STRING_AGG(DISTINCT cont."Type", '; ') as management_types
  FROM multiple_dwelling_registrations reg
  LEFT JOIN registration_contacts cont ON reg."RegistrationID" = cont."RegistrationID"
  WHERE cont."Type" IN ('Corporate Owner', 'Managing Agent', 'Head Officer')
    AND cont."CorporationName" IS NOT NULL 
  GROUP BY reg."BIN"
),

owner_properties AS (
  -- Count properties per owner for portfolio analysis
  SELECT 
    "Owner's Business Name" as owner_name,
    COUNT(*) as total_properties,
    SUM(CASE WHEN "Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN "Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer ELSE 0 END) as total_units
  FROM dob_now_applications
  WHERE "Owner's Business Name" IS NOT NULL
  GROUP BY "Owner's Business Name"
)

SELECT 
  -- Unique identifier
  proj.bin,
  
  -- Property details
  proj.full_address,
  proj.house_number,
  proj.street_name,
  proj.borough,
  proj.zip_code,
  proj.job_number,
  proj.filing_date,
  
  -- Project metrics
  CASE WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN proj.proposed_units_text::integer ELSE 0 END as dwelling_units,
  CASE WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' THEN proj.initial_cost_text::numeric ELSE 0 END as project_value,
  
  -- Owner information
  proj.primary_owner as developer_name,
  COALESCE(perm.permit_owner_business, proj.primary_owner) as contact_business_name,
  perm.owner_phone,
  perm.owner_location,
  
  -- Portfolio context
  portfolio.total_properties as owner_total_properties,
  portfolio.total_units as owner_total_units,
  
  -- Recent permit activity
  perm.latest_permit_type,
  perm.latest_permit_date,
  
  -- Current management status
  COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) as current_management_count,
  mgmt.current_managers,
  mgmt.management_types,
  
  -- CRM status fields (will be NULL initially - for CRM to populate)
  NULL::text as prospect_status,  -- 'INTERESTED', 'SELF_MANAGE', 'NOT_INTERESTED'
  NULL::boolean as starred,
  NULL::text as notes,
  NULL::timestamp as last_contact_date,
  NULL::text as seamless_enrichment_status,
  
  -- Flexible lead scoring
  CASE 
    -- Tier 1: Large developers (might outsource at scale)
    WHEN proj.primary_owner IN ('NAFTALI GROUP', 'EXTELL', 'RELATED', 'TOLL BROTHERS', 'AVALON BAY') 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 50 THEN 25
         
    -- Tier 2: High-value projects without management
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 100 
         AND COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 22
         
    -- Tier 3: Medium projects without management  
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 50 
         AND COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 18
         
    -- Tier 4: Smaller projects without management
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 25 
         AND COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 15
         
    -- Tier 5: Buildings with changeable management
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 25 
         AND mgmt.management_count = 1 THEN 12
         
    -- Tier 6: High-value projects (regardless of units)
    WHEN proj.initial_cost_text ~ '^[0-9.]+$' 
         AND proj.initial_cost_text::numeric >= 5000000 THEN 10
         
    -- Tier 7: Standard qualified leads
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 10 THEN 8
         
    ELSE 5
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Priority classification
  CASE
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 100 
         AND COALESCE(mgmt.management_count, 0) = 0 THEN 'IMMEDIATE'
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 50 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 25 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    WHEN proj.proposed_units_text ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND proj.proposed_units_text::integer >= 10 THEN 'QUALIFIED'
    ELSE 'RESEARCH'
  END as priority_tier,
  
  CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as generated_at

FROM project_data proj
LEFT JOIN most_recent_permits perm ON proj.bin = perm.bin
LEFT JOIN current_management mgmt ON proj.bin = mgmt.bin
LEFT JOIN owner_properties portfolio ON proj.primary_owner = portfolio.owner_name

ORDER BY lead_score DESC, dwelling_units DESC;
```

Claude: ## QUERY 43 - Simple Working Qualified Leads

```
-- Create working qualified leads (simplified without complex JOINs)
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW qualified_leads_simple AS
SELECT 
  -- Property identification
  app."Bin" as bin,
  app."Borough" as borough,
  app."House No" || ' ' || app."Street Name" as full_address,
  app."Postcode" as zip_code,
  app."Job Filing Number" as job_number,
  
  -- Project details
  app."Job Type" as job_type,
  app."Filing Date" as filing_date,
  CASE 
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer 
    ELSE 0 
  END as dwelling_units,
  CASE 
    WHEN app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' THEN app."Initial Cost"::numeric 
    ELSE 0 
  END as project_value,
  
  -- Owner information
  app."Owner's Business Name" as developer_name,
  
  -- Lead scoring
  CASE 
    -- Tier 1: Mega projects (500+ units)
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 500 THEN 30
         
    -- Tier 2: Large projects (100+ units)  
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 100 THEN 25
         
    -- Tier 3: Medium-large projects (50+ units)
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 50 THEN 20
         
    -- Tier 4: Medium projects (25+ units)
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 25 THEN 15
         
    -- Tier 5: Qualified projects (10+ units)
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 10 THEN 10
         
    -- Tier 6: High-value projects (regardless of units)
    WHEN app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' 
         AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric >= 10000000 THEN 15
    WHEN app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' 
         AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric >= 5000000 THEN 12
    WHEN app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' 
         AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric >= 1000000 THEN 8
         
    ELSE 5
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Priority classification
  CASE
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 500 THEN 'MEGA_PROJECT'
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 100 THEN 'IMMEDIATE'
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 50 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 25 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' 
         AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 10 THEN 'QUALIFIED'
    ELSE 'RESEARCH'
  END as priority_tier,
  
  -- CRM fields (NULL for now)
  NULL::text as prospect_status,
  NULL::boolean as starred,
  NULL::text as notes,
  
  CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as generated_at

FROM dob_now_applications app
WHERE 
  app."Job Type" = 'New Building'
  AND app."Owner's Business Name" IS NOT NULL
  AND app."Owner's Business Name" NOT IN ('SELF', 'PR', '')
  AND (
    -- 10+ units OR $1M+ value
    (app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$' AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 10)
    OR (app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric >= 1000000)
  )

ORDER BY lead_score DESC, dwelling_units DESC;
```

- ✅ BONA LLC (899 units, $37M)

## QUERY 45 - Create Working Qualified Leads View

```
-- Create the final working qualified leads view
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW qualified_leads AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  -- Property identification
  app."Bin" as bin,
  app."Borough" as borough,
  app."House No" || ' ' || app."Street Name" as full_address,
  app."House No" as house_number,
  app."Street Name" as street_name,
  app."Postcode" as zip_code,
  app."Job Filing Number" as job_number,
  
  -- Project details
  app."Job Type" as job_type,
  app."Filing Date" as filing_date,
  app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer as dwelling_units,
  CASE 
    WHEN app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' THEN app."Initial Cost"::numeric 
    ELSE 0 
  END as project_value,
  
  -- Owner information
  app."Owner's Business Name" as developer_name,
  
  -- Lead scoring (prioritize by units and value)
  CASE 
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 500 THEN 30  -- Mega projects
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 200 THEN 25  -- Large projects  
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 100 THEN 20  -- Medium-large
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 50 THEN 15   -- Medium
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 25 THEN 12   -- Small-medium
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 10 THEN 8    -- Qualified
    ELSE 5
  END +
  CASE 
    WHEN app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric >= 50000000 THEN 10
    WHEN app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric >= 10000000 THEN 5
    WHEN app."Initial Cost" ~ '^[0-9.]+$' AND app."Initial Cost"::numeric >= 5000000 THEN 3
    ELSE 0
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Priority classification
  CASE
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 500 THEN 'MEGA_PROJECT'
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 200 THEN 'IMMEDIATE'
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 100 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 50 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    WHEN app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 25 THEN 'QUALIFIED'
    ELSE 'RESEARCH'
  END as priority_tier,
  
  -- CRM status fields (for your app to populate)
  NULL::text as prospect_status,      -- 'INTERESTED', 'SELF_MANAGE', 'NOT_INTERESTED'
  NULL::boolean as starred,           -- TRUE for prospects you want to pursue
  NULL::text as notes,                -- Your notes about the prospect
  NULL::timestamp as last_contact,    -- Last contact date
  NULL::text as contact_method,       -- 'WHATSAPP', 'EMAIL', 'PHONE', 'MEETING'
  NULL::timestamp as next_followup,   -- Reminder date
  NULL::text as seamless_status,      -- 'ENRICHED', 'PENDING', 'FAILED'
  
  CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as generated_at

FROM dob_now_applications app
WHERE 
  app."Job Type" = 'New Building'
  AND app."Owner's Business Name" IS NOT NULL
  AND app."Owner's Business Name" NOT IN ('SELF', 'PR', '')
  AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units" IS NOT NULL
  AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units" ~ '^[0-9]+$'
  AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units"::integer >= 10  -- Minimum 10 units

ORDER BY lead_score DESC, dwelling_units DESC;
```

## QUERY 46 - Working Version with Actual Data

```
-- Create working view that matches our actual data
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW qualified_leads AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  app."Bin" as bin,
  app."Borough" as borough,
  app."House No" || ' ' || app."Street Name" as full_address,
  app."Owner's Business Name" as developer_name,
  app."Proposed Dwelling Units" as dwelling_units_text,
  CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) as dwelling_units,
  app."Initial Cost" as project_value_text,
  app."Filing Date" as filing_date,
  app."Job Filing Number" as job_number,
  
  -- Lead scoring based on units
  CASE 
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 500 THEN 30
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 200 THEN 25  
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 100 THEN 20
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 50 THEN 15
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 25 THEN 12
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 10 THEN 8
    ELSE 5
  END as lead_score,
  
  -- Priority tier
  CASE
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 500 THEN 'MEGA_PROJECT'
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 200 THEN 'IMMEDIATE'
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 100 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 50 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    WHEN CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 25 THEN 'QUALIFIED'
    ELSE 'RESEARCH'
  END as priority_tier

FROM dob_now_applications app
WHERE 
  app."Job Type" = 'New Building'
  AND app."Owner's Business Name" IS NOT NULL
  AND app."Owner's Business Name" NOT IN ('SELF', 'PR', '')
  AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units" IS NOT NULL
  AND app."Proposed Dwelling Units" != ''
  AND CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 10

ORDER BY CAST(app."Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) DESC;
```

-- Drop the view completely first
DROP VIEW qualified_leads;

-- Now create a fresh working version
CREATE VIEW qualified_leads AS
SELECT 
  "Bin" as bin,
  "Borough" as borough, 
  "House No" || ' ' || "Street Name" as address,
  "Owner's Business Name" as developer,
  "Proposed Dwelling Units" as units_text,
  CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) as units,
  "Initial Cost" as cost,
  "Filing Date" as filed,
  "Job Filing Number" as job_number,
  
  -- Simple scoring
  CASE 
    WHEN CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 500 THEN 'MEGA'
    WHEN CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 100 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 50 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    ELSE 'QUALIFIED'
  END as priority
  
FROM dob_now_applications 
WHERE "Job Type" = 'New Building'
  AND "Owner's Business Name" IS NOT NULL
  AND "Owner's Business Name" NOT IN ('SELF', 'PR', '')
  AND "Proposed Dwelling Units" IS NOT NULL
  AND CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 10
ORDER BY CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) DESC;
```

  "Bin" as bin_number,

    WHEN CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 50 THEN 'HIGH'
    WHEN CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 25 THEN 'MEDIUM'
    ELSE 'QUALIFIED'
  END as priority_level,

  CASE 
    WHEN CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 500 THEN 30
    WHEN CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 100 THEN 25
    WHEN CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 50 THEN 20
    WHEN CAST("Proposed Dwelling Units" AS INTEGER) >= 25 THEN 15
    ELSE 10

Claude: ## 🔧 METHOD 1: HubSpot Property Import (Easiest)

```
name,label,type,fieldType,options
bin_number,BIN Number,string,text,
property_address,Property Address,string,text,
dwelling_units,Dwelling Units,number,number,
project_value,Project Value,number,number,
priority_level,Priority Level,enumeration,select,"MEGA_PROJECT;IMMEDIATE;HIGH;MEDIUM;QUALIFIED"
lead_score,Lead Score,number,number,
prospect_status,Prospect Status,enumeration,select,"NEW;CONTACTED;INTERESTED;SELF_MANAGE;NOT_INTERESTED;STARRED"
last_contact_method,Last Contact Method,enumeration,select,"WHATSAPP;EMAIL;PHONE;MEETING;NONE"
filing_date,Filing Date,date,date,
job_number,Job Number,string,text,
seamless_status,Seamless Status,enumeration,select,"NOT_ENRICHED;ENRICHED;PENDING;FAILED"
next_followup,Next Follow-up,datetime,date,
```

1. Go to Settings → Properties → Company Properties
2. Click "Import"
3. Upload the CSV above
4. HubSpot will auto-create all fields

## 🚀 METHOD 2: HubSpot API Script (Fastest)

```
// HubSpot Custom Properties Creation Script
// Run this in browser console after getting API key

const properties = [
  {
    name: "bin_number",
    label: "BIN Number", 
    type: "string",
    fieldType: "text"
  },
  {
    name: "property_address",
    label: "Property Address",
    type: "string", 
    fieldType: "text"
  },
  {
    name: "dwelling_units",
    label: "Dwelling Units",
    type: "number",
    fieldType: "number"
  },
  {
    name: "project_value", 
    label: "Project Value",
    type: "number",
    fieldType: "number"
  },
  {
    name: "priority_level",
    label: "Priority Level",
    type: "enumeration",
    fieldType: "select",
    options: [
      {label: "MEGA PROJECT", value: "MEGA_PROJECT"},
      {label: "IMMEDIATE", value: "IMMEDIATE"}, 
      {label: "HIGH", value: "HIGH"},
      {label: "MEDIUM", value: "MEDIUM"},
      {label: "QUALIFIED", value: "QUALIFIED"}
    ]
  }
];

// You'll need your HubSpot API key to run this
```

Claude: ## 🏗️ PROPER CRM STRUCTURE:

### COMPANIES (Developers/Owners)

- Company Name (BONA LLC, LENDLEASE, etc.)
- Company Address
- Company Phone
- Website
- Total Properties Owned
- Total Units Managed
- Company Type (Developer, Owner, Management Co.)

### CONTACTS (People)

- First Name / Last Name
- Email / Phone
- Job Title
- Associated Company
- Contact Source (Seamless.ai, Manual, etc.)
- Last Contact Method

### DEALS/PROPERTIES (Projects)

- Property Address
- BIN Number
- Dwelling Units
- Project Value
- Priority Level
- Prospect Status
- Associated Company
- Filing Date

Human: I dont see where to import

Claude: ## 🔍 WHERE TO IMPORT IN HUBSPOT:

### OPTION 1: Main Import (Most Common)

1. Log into HubSpot
2. Click "Contacts" in top navigation
3. Click "Import" button (top right of screen)
4. Select "Start an import"

### OPTION 2: Via Settings

1. Click Settings gear icon (top right)
2. Go to "Data Management" → "Import & Export"
3. Click "Go to import"

### OPTION 3: Direct URL

- Go directly to: app.hubspot.com/import/{your-hub-id}/import

`app.hubspot.com/import/{your-hub-id}/import`

## 📝 IMPORT PROCESS:

### Step 1: Choose Import Type

- "File from computer" (for our CSV files)
- "One file" (we'll import companies first)

### Step 2: Select Object Type

- Choose "Companies" for first import
- Later: "Contacts" and "Deals" (for properties)

### Step 3: Upload CSV

- Upload our companies CSV file
- HubSpot will auto-detect columns
- Map our custom fields

## 🔧 HOW TO ADD THESE IN HUBSPOT:

### Step 1: Add Company Properties

1. Go to Settings → Properties → Company Properties
2. Click "Create property"
3. Add each property with these settings:

```
Property 1:
- Label: BIN Number
- Internal Name: bin_number  
- Type: Single-line text

Property 2:
- Label: Total Properties
- Internal Name: total_properties
- Type: Number

Property 3:
- Label: Total Units  
- Internal Name: total_units
- Type: Number

Property 4:
- Label: Company Type
- Internal Name: company_type
- Type: Dropdown select
- Options: Developer, Owner, Management Company

Property 5:
- Label: Prospect Status
- Internal Name: prospect_status
- Type: Dropdown select  
- Options: NEW, CONTACTED, INTERESTED, SELF_MANAGE, NOT_INTERESTED
```

### Step 2: Add Deal Properties (for Properties)

1. Go to Settings → Properties → Deal Properties
2. Add the property-specific fields

Human: ok, step by step how to add

Claude: ## 🔧 STEP-BY-STEP: Adding Company Properties

### STEP 1: Navigate to Properties

1. Log into your HubSpot account
2. Click the Settings gear icon (top right corner)
3. In the left sidebar, find "Data Management"
4. Click "Properties"
5. Click "Company properties"

### STEP 2: Create First Property - BIN Number

1. Click "Create property" button (top right)
2. Fill out the form:

```
Object type: Company
Property type: Single-line text
Label: BIN Number
Internal name: bin_number (will auto-populate)
Description: NYC Building Identification Number
Group: Company information
```

1. Click "Create" button

### STEP 3: Create Second Property - Total Properties

1. Click "Create property" again
2. Fill out:

```
Object type: Company  
Property type: Number
Label: Total Properties
Internal name: total_properties
Description: Total number of properties owned by this company
Group: Company information
```

1. Click "Create"

### STEP 4: Create Third Property - Total Units

1. Click "Create property" again
2. Fill out:

```
Object type: Company
Property type: Number  
Label: Total Units
Internal name: total_units
Description: Total dwelling units across all properties
Group: Company information
```

1. Click "Create"

### STEP 5: Create Fourth Property - Company Type

1. Click "Create property" again
2. Fill out:

```
Object type: Company
Property type: Dropdown select
Label: Company Type
Internal name: company_type
Description: Type of real estate company
Group: Company information
```

1. In the Options section, add these values:
   - Developer
   - Owner
   - Management Company
   - Government Agency
2. Click "Create"

### STEP 6: Create Fifth Property - Prospect Status

1. Click "Create property" one more time
2. Fill out:

```
Object type: Company
Property type: Dropdown select
Label: Prospect Status  
Internal name: prospect_status
Description: Current status of this prospect
Group: Company information
```

1. In the Options section, add these values:
   - NEW
   - CONTACTED
   - INTERESTED
   - SELF_MANAGE
   - NOT_INTERESTED
   - STARRED
2. Click "Create"

### STEP 1: Navigate to Company Properties

1. Log into HubSpot
2. Click Settings (gear icon in top right)
3. Go to: Data Management > Objects > Companies > Properties
4. Click "Create property"

### STEP 2: Create First Property - BIN Number

```
Property Label: BIN Number
Internal Name: bin_number (auto-fills)
Property Type: Single-line text
Description: NYC Building Identification Number for property tracking
Group: Company information
```

### STEP 3: Create Second Property - Total Properties

```
Property Label: Total Properties
Internal Name: total_properties
Property Type: Number
Description: Total number of properties owned by this company
Group: Company information
```

## 🔍 CURRENT STATUS CHECK:

- Logged into HubSpot?
- Can you see the Settings gear icon?
- Ready to start creating properties?

Human: CREATED. WHAT OTHER FEILDS DO WE NEED TO ADD? IN COMPANIES AND OTHER?

Claude: ## 🏢 COMPANY PROPERTIES (6 more to add):

### PROPERTY 2: Total Units

```
Property Label: Total Units
Internal Name: total_units
Property Type: Number
Description: Total dwelling units across all properties
Group: Company information
```

### PROPERTY 3: Company Type

```
Property Label: Company Type
Internal Name: company_type
Property Type: Dropdown select
Description: Type of real estate company
Group: Company information
Options: Developer, Owner, Management Company, Government Agency
```

### PROPERTY 4: Prospect Status

```
Property Label: Prospect Status
Internal Name: prospect_status
Property Type: Dropdown select
Description: Current status of this prospect
Group: Company information
Options: NEW, CONTACTED, INTERESTED, SELF_MANAGE, NOT_INTERESTED, STARRED
```

### PROPERTY 5: Data Source

```
Property Label: Data Source
Internal Name: data_source
Property Type: Single-line text
Description: Source of data (NYC API, Manual Entry, etc.)
Group: Company information
```

### PROPERTY 6: Last Data Update

```
Property Label: Last Data Update
Internal Name: last_data_update
Property Type: Date picker
Description: When this record was last updated from NYC data
Group: Company information
```

Claude: ## 🏗️ DEAL PROPERTIES (8 properties to add)

### STEP 1: Navigate to Deal Properties

1. Go to: Data Management > Objects > Deals > Properties
2. Click "Create property"

## DEAL PROPERTY 1: Property Address

```
Property Label: Property Address
Internal Name: property_address
Property Type: Single-line text
Description: Full building address
Group: Deal information
```

## DEAL PROPERTY 2: BIN Number

```
Property Label: BIN Number (Deal)
Internal Name: property_bin_number
Property Type: Single-line text
Description: NYC Building ID for this specific property
Group: Deal information
```

## DEAL PROPERTY 3: Dwelling Units

```
Property Label: Dwelling Units
Internal Name: dwelling_units
Property Type: Number
Description: Number of residential units in this building
Group: Deal information
```

## 🎯 START WITH THESE 3:

Human: Ok, whats next?

Claude: ## DEAL PROPERTY 4: Project Value

```
Property Label: Project Value
Internal Name: project_value
Property Type: Number
Description: Construction/development project cost
Group: Deal information
```

## DEAL PROPERTY 5: Priority Level

```
Property Label: Priority Level
Internal Name: priority_level
Property Type: Dropdown select
Description: Lead priority based on project size
Group: Deal information
Options: MEGA_PROJECT, IMMEDIATE, HIGH, MEDIUM, QUALIFIED
```

## DEAL PROPERTY 6: Filing Date

```
Property Label: Filing Date
Internal Name: filing_date
Property Type: Date picker
Description: When the NYC permit was filed
Group: Deal information
```

## DEAL PROPERTY 7: Job Number

```
Property Label: Job Number
Internal Name: job_number
Property Type: Single-line text
Description: NYC permit job number
Group: Deal information
```

## DEAL PROPERTY 8: Borough

```
Property Label: Borough
Internal Name: borough
Property Type: Dropdown select
Description: NYC Borough location
Group: Deal information
Options: MANHATTAN, BROOKLYN, QUEENS, BRONX, STATEN_ISLAND
```
